MGR review of GFX2: ACCEPT for the medium budget and the neon bounce; CONCERN on car paint, which does not reach the default cars. I ran tests/unit/render and entities: 31 files, 575 tests pass.
(1) Medium budget: MET. All seven views are under 500 (428 to 489), and only the golden aerial passes 480. GFX2 measured why: 13 full-detail cars inside the 72 m range at about 17 meshes each (body, glass, trim, lamps, four separate tyres). Merging far proxies cannot recover that. RULING on the headroom: keep the 72 m range and accept 489 for now. The fix belongs to whoever owns the car model: merge each near car's tyres into one mesh and its trim and lamps into one, which should save 5 to 8 draws per car, 60 or more on v3. That goes to the Stage 9 list under the car-model owner (SKINS this stage), not to GFX2.
(2) Neon bounce: MET within the band. One additive mesh washes facades and pools on the sidewalk under lit signs, shop glass and the three interior doors, stretching toward the street when wet. Phone draws none and low keeps 70 %. Dry streets stay in band at 22h (0.130) and midnight (0.102), with blown pixels at 0.03 % or less. GFX2 honestly reports the effect is subtle at 22h because the band leaves no room there; it shows best wet and at midnight. The wet street at 22h reads 0.145, over the band, but it was already 0.136 before this lane. DECISION for Fable: the night band was always measured dry; I recommend we state that in PRD §5.4 and let wet nights run up to about 0.15, because wet asphalt reflecting light is the GTA4 look we want.
(3) Car paint: CONCERN. CarPaintFx's clear coat (Fresnel rim, sky and street reflection, wetness) is wired into PaletteCarPaint only, and that material is used only by the Kenney glTF cars, which are opt-in behind VITE_GLTF_CARS=1. The default procedural cars (CARS2's bodies, now SKINS' SkinCarPaint) use the library's physical car paint, which already has a clear coat and picks up the sky environment at medium and high, but does not get GFX2's street-colour lower half or the wet boost. The before/after crop (crop_car4b.png) shows only a faint difference, which fits. So the GTA4 item 'car-paint reflections' is met by the existing physical clear coat, not by this lane's new code. FIX, small: SKINS (who owns SkinCarPaint) or GFX2 should splice CAR_PAINT_COAT's street and wet terms into SkinCarPaint's shader the same way PaletteCarPaint does, then show the dusk and night car views before and after at medium. GFX2 -> done; the car-paint splice moves to SKINS' review.

### 2026-11-09T14:28:25Z · MGR
MGR: stage9 plan written (docs/swarm/plans/stage9.md). In short:
Part A, GTA4 people and cars, is a level-of-detail chain. A new near step (people 2,000 to 3,000 triangles with edge loops at shoulders, elbows, knees, wrists, jaw and brow; cars 6,000 to 8,000 with real panels, arches, tyres and glass) sits on top of today's GTA3 bodies, which become the mid step and stay the nearest step on low and phone. Draw calls are the real limit, so near people drop to at most 3 meshes and near cars to at most 6, with detail moved into 512 px albedo, normal and roughness atlases. The near-car mesh merge comes first because it frees about 60 draws on the worst medium view. The user's 'cars modelled after actual cars' ask is a 14-type roster under CARHI: Crown Vic-type cab and old cruiser, Charger-type cruiser, Camry, Accord and Altima-type sedans, Civic-type compact, Explorer and Tahoe-type SUVs, Caravan-type livery cab, Sprinter-type van, NPR-type box truck, 40 ft city bus and a dollar van. Each is built to public spec-sheet proportions with parody names and invented badges, never real logos, and CARHI must source and correct my starting dimensions.
Part B, the map toward Bushwick: I chose a second, rotated grid stitched to ours at Broadway rather than rewriting every street as a polyline. Today's code assumes axis-aligned streets in about 20 places across 17 files, and a seam keeps all of that working inside each grid; one world-space lane and sidewalk graph joins them. Chunks of 2 by 2 blocks culled by distance keep the draw budget; the skyline ring moves out; traffic and pedestrians keep their per-tier counts and spawn around the player; every new block gets real PLUTO lots; mission POIs and interior doors must not move (tested). Phase B1 is orthogonal expansion south to Halsey and east to Broadway's line; phase B2 is Broadway, the Bushwick-side grid with Bushwick Ave, and the J/M el with a station house.
One decision for Fable and the user before B1: reaching Halsey from Greene passes several real streets we do not model, so we either insert them (my recommendation; the block count falls where it falls) or compress them. Lanes proposed: CARHI, PEDHI, LOD9 for part A; GEO2 (survey), GRID9, GEO3, ROADS9, EL9, MAP9 for part B, with file ownership, order, risks and gates in the plan.

### 2026-11-09T22:50:49Z · MGR
MGR review of PEDHI: ACCEPT on draws and structure; CONCERN on the look, which is not yet GTA4.
Verified: entities 435 green; one SkinnedMesh per LOD on a 12-bone rig, 1 draw per person. Draws are lower than without it (low view 1 320 -> 291, medium 440 -> 411; phone unchanged at 120/146). The SKINS defect is closed. Proportions are now realistic (about 7.5 heads), with fingers, shoes that read as shoes, painted faces and smooth shading; phone starts at mid.
Look (hi1/group-front, faces, the 4 m in-game close-up): a clear step away from Roblox, but at 4 m it reads as low-poly mannequins rather than GTA4. Specific gaps:
(1) Shoulders: square blocks stick out above the arms on every figure; the upper arm should come out of a rounded deltoid, not a box.
(2) Clothes are painted onto the body volume: the skirts flare, but jackets and hoodies have no thickness, collars, hems or folds; the normal maps do not show at this distance.
(3) Faces are flat masks: the eyes and mouths are painted on a near-flat front, with no eye sockets, nose bridge or cheekbones in silhouette.
(4) Hands hang as flat paddles.
(5) The lighting is flat; GTA4 people read through strong form shading on cheekbones, folds and shoulders.
Next pass (same draw budget): a rounded shoulder and deltoid loop; a jacket shell with hem and collar geometry; eye sockets, brow and nose in the head mesh; ASSETS' denim, knit and leather normal maps at 512; a judge frame at 2 m and 4 m in game at dusk.
Red at HEAD, not PEDHI's: world building-data (one collider per lot) and clutter (props off roadway) -> GEO3/GRID9 lot regeneration and CROWD/clutter owner.
